FNV scene submission gap-closure evidence, 2026-10-12.
Research only: no game execution, profiling, production hook or runtime acceptance.
Primary native interface: radare2 MCP. Automatic names/prototypes are not contract authority.
FNV: fnv_reverse/FalloutNV.exe, PE32 i386, preferred image base 00400000,
SHA-256 42fee7d6cd74e801372aa89c8f71c974cebd3c20ec9ad43d1465b8fa9646b49c.
Math provider: installed Lutris d3d_extras/v2/x32/d3dx9_38.dll copied read-only to
/tmp/fnv-d3dx9_38-audit.dll, PE32 i386, preferred image base 00400000,
SHA-256 743e77a228e7d75442263ad70051e44534f7972c6326fd34b505a9c2c245894b.
Provider captures use that provider's preferred VA, not FalloutNV addresses.
Its current runtime binding and selected CPU dispatch leaf were not observed.

Corrections: exploratory/misaligned prefixes are excluded from proof. At 0075FCDE
the provider uses 3DNow/FEMMS, not SSE. Qualified SSE leaf is 007633E7..0076352E
(328 bytes inclusive, RET 0C at 0076352C); 91 instructions, 16 MULPS, 12 ADDPS.
PPLight's own constants initializer is BD4D30, not inherited B7E430.
WorldViewProjTranspose belongs to global ShadowLight +34; PPLight +34 registers
TreeData/WindMatrices instead. E8F200 passes Lock destination first to memcpy;
its old clipped pseudocode does not establish the reverse copy direction.
Renderer lock wrappers do not publish NiCriticalSection auxiliary owner fields.
A lock's existence is not complete proof of all transform writer exclusion.

Primary numeric constraint: Intel IA-32 SDM vol.1 order 253665-021,
sections 10.2.3.1-10.2.3.4, 11.6.6:
https://www.intel.com/content/dam/support/us/en/documents/processors/pentium4/sb/25366521.pdf
Bits 0..5 are sticky SIMD status; 7..12 masks; control includes RC, DAZ, FTZ.
Only all-masked audited SSE-leaf reuse is designed. x87 and 3DNow retain native work.
No source-schema, static byte match or operation count is a behavioral test.
